It is the object\nthat all API calls use to create Meters, instruments, and ultimately make\nmetric measurements. Also, it is an object that should be used to control the\nlife-cycle (start, flush, and shutdown) of the SDK.\n\nA MeterProvider needs to be configured to export the measured data,\nthis is done by configuring it with a Reader implementation (using the\nWithReader MeterProviderOption). Readers take two forms: ones that push to an\nendpoint (NewPeriodicReader), and ones that an endpoint pulls from. See the\ngo.opentelemetry.io/otel/exporters package for exporters that can be used as or\nwith these Readers.\n\nEach Reader, when registered with the MeterProvider, can be augmented with a\nView. Views allow users that run OpenTelemetry instrumented code to modify the\ngenerated data of that instrumentation.\n\nThe data generated by a MeterProvider needs to include information about its\norigin. A MeterProvider needs to be configured with a Resource, using the\nWithResource MeterProviderOption, to include this information. This Resource\nshould be used to describe the unique runtime environment instrumented code is\nbeing run on. That way when multiple instances of the code are collected at a\nsingle endpoint their origin is decipherable.\n\nvar ErrExporterShutdown = fmt.Errorf(\"exporter is shutdown\")\nvar
